We combine controlled history, profile, and event comparisons with independent plan evaluation. These assess safe resolution, cost efficiency under user priorities, and responses to contact events. Evaluation of LLM-based and symbolic planners shows that safely completing a task does not guarantee the lowest execution costs under the user's priorities. To address this problem, we introduce Hygiene-NSP. It combines LLM-based grounding, contact-history reconstruction, and CP-SAT to jointly plan hygiene treatment and task execution under user priorities. Hygiene-NSP achieves safe resolution and optimal safe resolution rates of 94.4% and 90.4%, respectively. Both rates are higher than those of the evaluated baseline planners on the full dataset. Project page: https://euron-zc.github.io/HygieneRoboBench/.

</details>


### [109] [Fast Non-Parametric Heteroscedastic Imitation Learning With Geometric Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08650)
*Maximilian Mühlbauer,Arne Sachtler,Markus Knauer,Cem Küçükgenç,Yanlong Huang,Alin Albu-Schäffer,João Silvério*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用几何先验的非参数概率建模方法，在数据稀缺和异方差不确定性场景下学习概率策略，支持流形输入与输出、大姿态变化，并可在3ms内完成轨迹更新，实现快速适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从人类演示中学习概率策略需要数据高效学习与快速适应新场景。现有非参数（多为核方法）方案要么忽略机器人中常见的流形几何，限制数据效率；要么虽具几何感知能力，却提供不可靠的不确定性估计或需要重训练才能适应。

Method: 提出一种非参数方法，在数据稀缺与异方差不确定性场景中引入几何先验进行概率建模。基于时间或机器人状态构建策略，采用不可分离对角核以捕捉相同维度输入输出间各自由度（DOF）的不确定性关系；通过优化公式实现快速更新。方法同时支持流形值的输入与输出，并能处理大姿态变化；借助任务参数化实现对不同物体位姿的适应。

Result: 包含位置与姿态的轨迹更新所需时间少于3 ms；在一组玩具示例及真实机器人操作任务（自主执行与共享控制场景）上进行了评估。

Conclusion: 该方法结合几何先验与不可分离对角核，在数据稀缺条件下实现高效、可靠的概率策略学习与快速适应，适用于流形输入输出的机器人任务。

Abstract: When learning probabilistic policies from human demonstrations, data-efficient learning and fast adaptations to new scenarios are key requirements. One popular way to achieve intuitive and reliable adaptations is through non-parametric, typically kernel-based, methods. However, existing solutions either fail to account for the geometry of manifolds common in robotics, limiting data efficiency, or, when geometry-aware, provide unreliable uncertainty estimates or require retraining to adapt. We propose a non-parametric approach leveraging geometric priors in scenarios of data scarcity and heteroscedastic uncertainties for probabilistic modeling. We utilize the method to formulate policies based on time or robot state, where non-separable diagonal kernels allow capturing uncertainty relations between degrees of freedom for same-sized in- and outputs. Fast updates, requiring less than 3 ms for a trajectory involving both position and orientation are possible through an optimized formulation. Our approach supports both manifold-valued input and manifold-valued output with large orientation changes. Using task parameterization, adaptation to different object poses is easily possible. We evaluate the approach on a set of toy examples and on real robot manipulation tasks both in autonomous execution and in shared control scenarios.

</details>


### [110] [Magnet-Aware Control of Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08653)
*J. Playan Garai,S. B. Djuve,C. McGreavy,M. Khadiv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一套面向足式机器人的磁场感知控制框架，通过建模、估计与动态补偿空间变化磁力/磁矩干扰，显著提升机器人在强磁场环境中的稳定运行范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大科学装置中自主机器人可提升运行时间并减少人员暴露，但强磁场会损坏传感器并产生随位姿变化的机械力矩，干扰机器人稳定性，使传统反应式控制器难以应对。

Method: 1) 为MuJoCo开发自定义物理插件，对刚体单元上的磁力进行建模；2) 提出逆场估计框架，从四足机器人动力学响应及任意数量传感器读数中推断潜在磁场；3) 构建磁场感知的模型预测控制（MPC）与全身控制（WBC）架构，在运动过程中预测并抵消磁扰动。

Result: 经仿真与实机验证：相比未补偿系统，在力空间中最大可抑制磁场扰动提升2.5倍，在力矩空间提升1.6–2倍；在给定磁场内，机器人可运行区域增加29.34%，其中10.84%此前会导致未补偿控制器立即失稳崩塌。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效建模、估计并动态补偿磁场干扰，扩大足式机器人在强磁场环境中的可运行范围，为大科学设施中的自主机器人应用提供可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous robots can increase uptime and reduce human exposure in Big Science facilities, but strong magnetic fields needed for their operation corrupt sensors and induce pose-dependent mechanical wrenches that destabilize robots and challenge conventional reactive controllers. This paper presents a control framework for modeling, estimating, and dynamically compensating for spatially varying magnetic wrenches acting on legged robots to improve robustness in these fields. We introduce a custom physics plugin for the MuJoCo simulator to model magnetic forces on rigid-body elements, alongside an inverse field-estimation framework to infer the latent magnetic field directly from quadruped dynamic responses and any number of sensor readings. Furthermore, we develop a Magnet-Aware Model Predictive Control (MPC) and Whole-Body Control (WBC) architecture that predicts and counteracts magnetic perturbations during locomotion to increase the range of magnetic fields in which the robot can operate. The effectiveness of the framework is validated through both simulation and physical hardware experiments. We show our method increases the the maximum rejectable disturbances from magnetic field in the force space by a factor of 2.5 and and between 1.6-2 times in torque space compared to a non-compensated system. Within the proposed magnetic field, this constitutes an increase in the area in which the robot can operate by 29,34% of which 10,84% would have previously caused an immediate collapse to non-compensated controllers.

</details>


### [111] [NMPP: Nonlinear Model Predictive Planning for Agile UAV Flight in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08695)
*Ondřej Procházka,Juraj Pauko,Robert Pěnička,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出非线性模型预测规划（NMPP），将感知障碍作为硬几何约束并输出全状态参考给盲SE(3)控制器，在精度、速度与成功率上显著优于线性MPC与多项式轨迹规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼穿越杂乱环境时，参考轨迹不仅要无碰撞，还需动态可行且满足执行器限制，以便控制器精确跟踪。现有方法存在不足：一类在凸走廊内优化光滑多项式，限制敏捷性；另一类将障碍作为软代价，与跟踪性能相互折中。

Method: 提出非线性模型预测规划（NMPP）：将感知到的障碍施加为硬几何约束，并向不感知障碍的SE(3)控制器提供全状态参考轨迹，从而实现规划与跟踪的解耦。

Result: 相比线性MPC轨迹规划器位置RMSE降低58–67%，相比多项式轨迹规划器降低41–70%；在森林飞行中以最高9.5 m/s速度无碰撞完成全部飞行；在更激进速度剖面下成功率达86%，而某先进规划器仅26%；实际部署在未知杂乱环境中可靠飞行至5.5 m/s。

Conclusion: 以硬几何约束替代软代价、并配合盲SE(3)控制器的全状态参考，可同时兼顾敏捷性、动态可行性与跟踪精度，在仿真与真实环境中均验证了其优越性与可靠性。

Abstract: Flying a quadrotor through a cluttered environment requires not only planning a collision-free reference trajectory based on perceived obstacles, but the reference also needs to be dynamically feasible and within the actuation limits of the vehicle, so that the controller can track it precisely. Existing methods either optimize a smooth polynomial inside a convex corridor, which limits agility, or treat obstacles as soft costs traded against tracking performance. We propose a Nonlinear Model Predictive Planning (NMPP) that imposes perceived obstacles as hard geometric constraints and hands a full-state reference to an obstacle-blind SE(3) controller. Our planner achieves a 58-67 % lower position RMSE than a linear Model Predictive Control trajectory planner and a 41-70 % lower RMSE than a polynomial trajectory planner. It also completes all forest flights with up to 9.5 m/s speed without collisions, and achieves 86 % flight success rate under a more aggressive speed profile where a state-of-the-art planner has only 26 % success rate. The real-world deployment showed reliable execution flying up to 5.5 m/s in an unknown cluttered environment.

</details>


### [112] [EgoLAP: Learning from Egocentric Human Data through Language-Action Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08726)
*Lihan Zha,Shresth Grover,Tenny Yin,Samuel M. Bateman,Hengkai Pan,Mengchao Zhang,Aykut Onol,Allen Z. Ren,Dhruv Shah,Anirudha Majumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoLAP：通过共享的语言化动作思维链，联合利用人类与机器人轨迹进行VLA预训练，以运动意图跨越具身差异，实现人类经验向机器人控制的高效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心人类数据可突破昂贵机器人示范的规模瓶颈，但具身差异使原始人类轨迹难以直接作为控制监督目标，亟需一种能跨具身迁移的有效动作表示。

Method: 提出EgoLAP，一种VLA预训练框架：以共享的、基于语言的动作思维链（action chain-of-thought）联合学习人类与机器人轨迹；将运动意图表达为结构化、时间抽象的语言动作，并与基于场景几何、物理与物体可供性的运动级推理相配对。

Result: 在大量真实与仿真实验中，EgoLAP比替代动作表示更有效地将人类经验迁移到机器人控制，真实任务平均进度达80.1%，相比替代动作表示提升2.3倍；运动级推理亦优于结合子任务、物体框与视觉轨迹的复合推理格式。

Conclusion: 低层动作虽具身相关，但其底层运动意图可捕捉跨人类与机器人可迁移的任务相关结构；以语言化运动意图为核心的动作表示是实现人类数据规模化赋能机器人学习的有效路径。

Abstract: Egocentric human data offer a path to scaling robot learning beyond costly robot demonstrations, yet the embodiment gap makes raw human trajectories a poor supervisory target for control. Our key insight is that, although low-level actions are embodiment-specific, their underlying motion intent can capture task-relevant structure that transfers across humans and robots. We introduce EgoLAP, a VLA pre-training framework that jointly learns from human and robot trajectories through a shared language-based action chain-of-thought. EgoLAP expresses motion intent as structured, temporally abstracted language actions and pairs them with motion-level reasoning grounded in scene geometry, physics, and object affordances. Across extensive real-world and simulated experiments, EgoLAP transfers human experience to robot control more effectively than alternative action representations and reaches 80.1% mean real-world task progress, a 2.3x performance gain over alternative action representations. Motion-level reasoning also outperforms a composite reasoning format that combines subtask, object-box, and visual-trace reasoning.

</details>


### [113] [Towards an Extensible Benchmark for Spoken Dialogue with Social Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08733)
*Casey Kennington,Ross Mead,Saad Elbeleidy,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个面向人机口语对话的基准测试，并引入实时通信框架Retico以支撑机器人实时语音交互能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型虽为人类与机器人提供了即插即用接口，但在语音、对话、快速交互与协作场景下仍面临重要挑战；社区缺乏用于探索机器人与人类之间常见口语对话现象的统一评估手段。

Method: 提出一个基准测试，用于探索人机口语对话中的典型现象，包括澄清请求、打断、具身信号（如点头或面部线索）以及时间约束；并提出将该基准扩展到更广泛的人机交互研究方向。同时提出使用实时通信框架Retico来满足机器人实现口语对话能力所需的关键技术要求。

Result: 未在摘要中给出具体实验结果；主要贡献为基准测试的提出、其扩展愿景以及Retico实时通信框架的引入。

Conclusion: 该工作旨在为社区提供可复用的基准与实时通信框架，以推动机器人在语音、快速交互与协作场景下的口语对话研究，并计划扩展至更多人机交互关键方面。

Abstract: Language models provide a plug-and-play interface between humans and robots, but important challenges remain when speech, dialogue, fast interaction, and collaboration are required. We propose a benchmark for the community to use as a way to explore common spoken dialogue artifacts between robots and humans, including requests for clarification, interruptions, embodied signals (e.g., head nods or facial cues), and time constraints. We also explain our vision to extend the benchmark for other aspects of human-robot interaction that are important to the larger research community. To facilitate the benchmark, we further propose using \textit{Retico}, a real-time communication framework that fulfills important technical requirements to enable robots to have spoken dialogue capabilities.

</details>


### [114] [PhoneBot: A Low-Cost Open Humanoid Robot Platform Reusing Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08737)
*Ruochen Hou,Quanyou Wang,Daniel Koh,Dennis W. Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhoneBot：以智能手机为感知与计算核心的低成本开源人形机器人平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在教育科研中普及受限，主要因硬件成本高、传感系统复杂、算力需求大；本文旨在通过复用商用智能手机降低门槛。

Method: 以智能手机作为主要感知与计算单元，利用其IMU、摄像头、无线连接与板载算力；机械上采用13个低成本执行器驱动的模块化下肢结构，躯干安装智能手机承担感知、控制计算与交互；并设计机械结构、软件架构与实时通信框架以支持稳定行走。

Result: 实验验证了可靠行走、感知驱动交互，并可在现成消费级智能手机上直接部署；实现视觉人跟随、对话交互、拍摄与移动遥呈现等功能。

Conclusion: PhoneBot以全开源软硬件提供可负担、可复现的平台，适用于教育、研究与快速原型开发，详情见 https://phonebot.dev。

Abstract: The adoption of humanoid robots in education and research remains limited by high hardware costs, complex sensing systems, and substantial computational requirements. This paper presents PhoneBot, a low-cost, open-source humanoid robot platform that repurposes commodity smartphones as its primary sensing and computing unit. By using a smartphone's integrated inertial measurement unit (IMU), camera, wireless connectivity, and onboard processing capabilities, PhoneBot reduces hardware costs and simplifies the system architecture. The robot combines a modular lower-body structure driven by 13 low-cost actuators with a torso-mounted smartphone that supports perception, control computation, and user interaction. We describe the mechanical design, software architecture, and real-time communication framework that support stable locomotion and capabilities including vision-based human following, conversational interaction, filming, and mobile telepresence. Experimental evaluations demonstrate reliable walking, perception-driven interaction, and straightforward deployment using off-the-shelf consumer smartphones. With fully open-source hardware and software designs, PhoneBot provides an affordable, reproducible platform for education, research, and rapid prototyping. More details are available at https://phonebot.dev.

</details>


### [115] [LBA-CBF: Rapidly Adaptive Safety Filters via Parallel Dynamics Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08765)
*Maitham F. AL-Sunni,Timeea-Andreea Radu,Hassan Almubarak,Henry Z. Liao,Michael Görner,Francesco Maurelli,John M. Dolan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Look-Back Adaptive Control Barrier Functions (LBA-CBF)，通过对候选动力学模型库进行短期回溯预测误差排序，并对最优模型容差范围内的所有模型施加高阶CBF约束，从而在参数未知、非线性依赖且发生突变工况时保持安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CBF依赖假定的动力学模型来认证控制指令，当系统发生突发且不可测的工况变化（如风速反、负载变化）时，模型失配会破坏安全证书，而这恰恰是安全最关键的时刻。现有自适应或鲁棒方法要么需要连续参数化估计器，要么依赖切换模型，难以应对非线性参数依赖与突发变化。

Method: 方法维护一个有限候选动力学模型库，利用短回溯窗口内的近期预测误差对模型进行排序；针对误差在最优模型容差范围内的所有模型统一施加高阶CBF条件，从而在“最佳拟合自适应”与“全库鲁棒滤波”之间连续过渡。动力学可非线性依赖未知参数，无需切换模型或连续参数化估计器。理论证明：只要保留一个具有安全代表性的候选模型，任何可行滤波输入都满足真实CBF条件。

Result: 在含突发风向反转和未知负载的四旋翼仿真中，LBA-CBF在所有随机初始条件下均安全并到达目标，性能与oracle相当；自适应与鲁棒基线成功率仅为0-88%。模型库规模可达250,000个并在控制回路内实时运行。Crazyflie 2.1与F1TENTH实物实验验证了其对风扰、负载释放及变化的轮胎-路面摩擦的适应能力。

Conclusion: LBA-CBF通过回溯误差驱动的有限模型库排序与容差内多模型高阶CBF约束，在不依赖连续估计器或切换模型的前提下实现了对突发工况变化的可靠安全认证，兼具理论保证与大规模实时可行性，并在仿真与实物平台上验证有效。

Abstract: Control barrier functions (CBFs) certify commands through an assumed dynamics model, so an abrupt, unmeasured regime change can undermine the certificate exactly when safety matters most. We present Look-Back Adaptive Control Barrier Functions (LBA-CBF), which rank a finite bank of candidate dynamics by recent prediction error over a short look-back window and enforce the high-order CBF condition against every model within a tolerance of the best, spanning best-fit adaptation to full-bank robust filtering. The dynamics may depend nonlinearly on the unknown parameters, and no switching model or continuously parameterized estimator is required. We prove that any feasible filtered input satisfies the true CBF condition whenever a safety-representative candidate is retained. In quadrotor simulation with abrupt wind reversals and an unknown payload, LBA-CBF is safe and reaches the goal from all random initial conditions, matching an oracle, while adaptive and robust baselines achieve 0-88% success. Banks of up to 250,000 models run inside the control loop, and Crazyflie 2.1 and F1TENTH experiments demonstrate adaptation to wind, payload release, and varying tire-road friction. Code, videos, and project details are available at: https://lla-control.github.io

</details>


### [116] [DepthWorld: 3D World Model for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08780)
*Jai Bardhan,Josef Sivic,Vladimir Petrik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DROID-3D标定数据集与DepthWorld世界模型，通过多视角RGB与深度联合预测，使视频世界模型具备一致的3D几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的世界模型仅用RGB训练，逐帧看似正确但无法构成一致的3D世界，缺乏忠实3D几何，难以支撑机器人策略评估、改进与规划。缩小差距需两方面进展：大规模机器人操作3D监督数据，以及能吸收该监督而不破坏预训练视频先验的架构。

Method: 提出标定流程：结合学习到的立体深度与联合因子图，将同一物理机器人采集的所有episode汇聚，恢复其共享运动学参数及逐场景外参。应用于DROID数据集得到DROID-3D，提供密集度量深度与重标定多视角外参。基于Stable Video Diffusion训练DepthWorld世界模型，通过空间潜在分块（spatial latent tiling）联合预测多视角RGB与深度，保持预训练VAE不变。

Result: DROID-3D在90%的episode上外部相机重投影误差<0.7 px。DepthWorld在相同训练预算下，相比纯RGB基线，RGB预测PSNR提升+1.48 dB，同时输出准确度量深度用于下游几何推理。

Conclusion: 联合深度监督不仅不损害预训练视频先验，反而提升RGB预测质量，并赋予世界模型度量3D几何能力，为机器人世界模型的几何一致性提供数据与架构双重方案。

Abstract: World models offer a data-driven alternative to traditional simulators for robotics, with applications spanning policy evaluation, improvement, and planning. All of these uses depend on faithful 3D geometry, yet current video-based world models are trained on RGB alone and produce rollouts that look correct frame-by-frame but do not compose into a consistent 3D world. Closing this gap requires progress on two fronts: large-scale 3D supervision for manipulation, and an architecture that can absorb it without disturbing strong pretrained video priors. We introduce a calibration pipeline that combines learned stereo depth with a joint factor graph, pooling all episodes collected from the same physical robot to recover its shared kinematic parameters alongside per-scene extrinsics. Applied to the DROID dataset, this yields DROID-3D, a calibrated 3D dataset providing dense metric depth and recalibrated multi-view extrinsics (achieving <0.7 px reprojection error on 90% of episodes for external cameras). We then train DepthWorld, a Stable Video Diffusion-based world model that jointly predicts multi-view RGB and depth via spatial latent tiling, leaving the pretrained Variational Autoencoder (VAE) unchanged. Depth supervision improves RGB prediction itself by +1.48 dB PSNR over an identical RGB-only baseline at equal training budget, while simultaneously yielding accurate metric depth for downstream geometric reasoning.

</details>


### [117] [PEARS: Physical-Prior-Guided Efficient Adaptation via Failure Reasoning and Diffusion Steering for Tactile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08784)
*Kun Song,Yiming Wang,Yilin Chen,Tianyi Ding,Jiaxin Tian,Tianqi Gong,Daolin Ma,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PEARS：一个物理先验引导的混合强化学习框架，利用触觉反馈实现预训练策略的高样本效率在线自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略在部署中遇到分布外（OOD）条件时性能显著下降，需要真实交互后训练；但基于强化学习的后训练通常需要大量环境交互，尤其在操作任务中每次试验慢、昂贵甚至具破坏性。因此需要高样本效率的在线自适应方法。

Method: PEARS包含三部分：1）物理引导力推理（PFR）模块，每个episode后借助VLM中编码的物理先验，从视觉结果和触觉交互历史诊断失败，并更新任务适配的接触力边界；2）高频混合力-位置控制器，在接触阶段执行这些力边界；3）触觉条件扩散引导强化学习，通过调整冻结flow-matching策略的潜在噪声，纠正自由空间运动与接触时机误差，而不更新基础模型。

Result: 仿真中，PEARS相较各任务最强基线成功率提升12.4–37.4个百分点；达到既定成功率阈值所需交互episode数相较最快基线最多减少53.2%。真实实验中，白板擦除成功率95%，移液管液体吸取成功率90%。

Conclusion: 将PFR模块与策略引导相结合，可在减少昂贵交互的同时加速预训练策略的自适应，验证了物理先验与触觉反馈融合的有效性。

Abstract: Pretrained robotic policies can suffer substantial performance degradation under out-of-distribution (OOD) conditions encountered during deployment, motivating post-training through real-world interaction. However, reinforcement-learning (RL)-based post-training typically requires substantial environment interactions, a burden that is especially significant in manipulation, where each trial can be slow, costly, or destructive. Therefore, we present PEARS, a physics-prior-guided hybrid RL framework for sample-efficient online adaptation of pretrained policies with tactile feedback. After each episode, its physics-guided force reasoning (PFR) module uses physical priors encoded in a vision-language model (VLM) to diagnose failures from the visual outcome and tactile interaction history and update task-appropriate contact-force bounds. A high-frequency hybrid force-position controller then enforces these bounds during contact. Complementarily, tactile-conditioned diffusion steering reinforcement learning adjusts the latent noise of the frozen flow-matching policy to correct errors in free-space motion and contact timing without updating the base model. In simulation, PEARS improves success rates by 12.4-37.4 percentage points over the strongest per-task baselines. PEARS also reduces the number of interaction episodes required for a certain success threshold by up to 53.2% relative to the fastest baseline. In real-world experiments, PEARS achieves success rates of 95% on Whiteboard Erasing and 90% on Pipette Liquid Aspiration. These results show that combining the PFR module with policy steering can accelerate adaptation while reducing costly interactions. The project website is available at https://song-kun.github.io/pears.

</details>


### [118] [QF3: Fast Flow RL with Filtered Q-Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08789)
*Chung Min Kim,Brent Yi,David McAllister,Hongsuk Choi,Himanshu Gaurav Singh,Jinkun Cao,Ken Goldberg,Pieter Abbeel,Carmelo Sferrazza,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QF3：通过一步流输出预测反传评论家动作梯度，并以"仅更新接近回放动作的维度"过滤该梯度，实现高效离线策略（off-policy）流策略强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流策略（flow policies）已成为从示范中学习机器人行为的标准方法，但强化学习对于改进预训练流策略或从零通过交互学习仍不可或缺；已有流强化学习方法多为在线策略（on-policy），样本效率与训练速度受限。

Method: 提出QF3（Fast Flow RL with Filtered Q-Gradients）在线off-policy算法：结合流匹配（flow matching）与评论家（critic）的动作梯度，梯度经由流输出的一步预测反传；为保证更新可靠性，仅对保持在回放动作附近的动作维度施加评论家梯度（过滤Q梯度）；并配合高吞吐off-policy训练配方。

Result: 据称是首个能从头训练人形机器人运动策略并零样本迁移到硬件的off-policy流强化学习方法；人形运动与运动跟踪任务训练相比on-policy流强化学习FPO++实现10倍墙钟加速；在ABC-Sim与Robomimic任务上微调预训练流式操作策略亦有效。

Conclusion: QF3既能从零学习机器人策略，也能精炼从示范中获得的策略，兼具off-policy高效性与流策略表达力。

Abstract: Flow policies have become a standard policy class for learning robot behaviors from demonstrations, but reinforcement learning is still critical for improving pre-trained flow policies or learning them from scratch through interaction. We introduce QF3 (Fast Flow RL with Filtered Q-Gradients), an online off-policy RL algorithm that trains a flow policy with flow matching plus the critic's action gradient, backpropagated through a one-step prediction of the flow's output. To keep updates where the critic and this prediction are reliable, QF3 applies the critic gradient only to action dimensions that stay near the replay action. To our knowledge, QF3 is the first off-policy flow RL method to train humanoid locomotion policies from scratch and transfer them zero-shot to hardware. Paired with a high-throughput off-policy training recipe, it trains humanoid locomotion and motion-tracking policies with a 10x wall-clock speedup over FPO++, a recent on-policy flow RL method. We further apply QF3 to fine-tune pretrained flow-based manipulation policies on both ABC-Sim and Robomimic tasks. These results suggest that QF3 can both learn robot policies from scratch and refine those acquired from demonstrations. Website: https://qf3-rl.github.io/

</details>
